# Thief Agent — TODO (atomic, execution-ready checklist)

> Repository: `thief/` · Natural role: **THIEF** · Team: `amireman` · Package: `thief_agent`
> Companion to `thief/docs/PRD.md` and `thief/docs/PLAN.md`. Regenerated in Phase 2.1 to full Vibe-Coding-Lifecycle depth.
> Status vocabulary: `READY` · `BLOCKED` · `BLOCKED-EXTERNAL` · `DONE`.
> Priority — **P0** legally-playable & avoid technical losses · **P1** strong counted-match readiness · **P2** advanced research/optimization/optional.
> Sprint — **Day-1**/**Day-2**/**Day-3**/**Post-core** (see the Three-Day Critical Execution Path in PLAN.md).
> Every Python file: **maximum 150 physical lines** (CI-enforced, no bypass). Task fields: ID · objective · Files · Deps · Test · Evidence · Acceptance · Priority · Sprint · Status.
> Role note: natural THIEF on sub-games 1/3/5, swapped on 2/4/6 — this repo is a full dual-role agent. Sibling repo: `police/`.

## Named acceptance gates (dependency roll-ups)
`G-MINPLAYABLE` legal local play · `G-PRACTICE` uncounted networked practice · `G-COUNTEDREADY` counted-match readiness · `G-SUBMISSION` final submission · `G-POLBRAINS`/`G-THFBRAINS` portfolios · `G-POL90`/`G-THF90` 90% evaluation · `G-EXPLOIT` no-collapse.

- `official_signer` steps — **BLOCKED-EXTERNAL** (lecturer's Step-0 pre-provided signing key).
- `oauth_setup`, `counted_send` — **BLOCKED** (team-controlled fresh `gmail.send` OAuth creation; real send also awaits an opponent + readiness gate).

## Day-2 Part 2 delivery — P12–P17 (2026-07-31) — DONE

Advanced adaptive agents delivered as cohesive themed modules (each ≤150 lines,
each routed through the existing legality firewall + guaranteed fallback). The
FR-STRATEGY-POLICE-* / FR-STRATEGY-THIEF-* mechanisms in sections H2/M/M2/N are
implemented and covered by the modules and tests below rather than as 60 separate
single-mechanism files; acceptance is proven by the cited tests.

- **P12 opponents + evaluation** — DONE · `sim/opponents/{simple,tricky,reference,registry}.py`
  (greedy, random, shortest-path, mobility, corner/trap, barrier-heavy, deceptive,
  reference), `sim/{metrics,evaluation,tournament}.py`, CLI `tournament`. Held-out
  split (tuning vs held-out opponents), reproducible seeds, metrics (capture/survival/
  score/illegal/diagonal/fallback/timeouts/decision-time p50/p95, per-opponent,
  per-role). Tests: `tests/unit/test_opponents_eval.py`.

Evidence (held-out, seeds 1..8): Police champion = **barrier** (capture_rate 0.594);
Thief champion = **endgame** (survival_rate 1.0). Baselines far lower (Police
tuning capture_rate 0.125–0.375). Re-run: `uv run python -m thief_agent tournament`.

counted matches, final submission docs/screenshots.

## Day-2 Part 3 delivery — P20 Replay Viewer + P21 GUI (2026-07-31) — DONE

Text-based, headless-testable local visualization (no external GUI deps).

- **P20 Replay Viewer** — DONE · `gui/replay_data.py` (reconstruct frames from AUDITED
  records, malformed-log safe handling, `load_log`, `board_at`, post-audit truth board),
  `gui/replay_verify.py` (per-step SHA-256 verification + config-hash check),
  `gui/replay_controls.py` (VERIFIED OK / TAMPERED display + stepper bounds),

  Tests: `tests/unit/test_replay.py`.
- **P21 GUI** — DONE · `gui/board_view.py` (local-truth board + scent overlay),
  `gui/heatmap.py` (belief heatmap), `gui/status_banner.py` (banner + input lock),

The live GUI shows only the local player's truth (exactly one player marker) and cannot
reveal the opponent's position; the replay viewer shows both trajectories from
post-audit revealed records. Still deferred (out of scope): GUI screenshots
